# Content trace: India and the Allied Industrial Base

Each claim of the brief with the dashboard field it comes from, the source datasets and the evidence quality it rests on.

**s1.1** India is a partner in the trusted network behind the Allied Industrial Base, not a core member, yet it already holds a part in all of it.

- `aib.network.tier` = a partner in the trusted network | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Country strategy choices by technology and supply-chain step (supply-chain need, Strategic Industrial Value, Competitive Position, deployment need, policy alignment), computed by the Explorer's authors | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the trusted network (core members, partners and economies treated as allied), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run

**s1.2** It holds a role in all ten strategic compacts and core seats in Solar Manufacturing, Wind & Hydropower Equipment, Nuclear Fuel & Reactor Systems, Grid Equipment & Conductors and Industrial Electrification & Hydrogen.

- `aib.n_compact_roles` = ten | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the trusted network (core members, partners and economies treated as allied), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run
- `aib.core_in` = Solar Manufacturing, Wind & Hydropower Equipment, Nuclear Fuel & Reactor Systems, Grid Equipment & Conductors and Industrial Electrification & Hydrogen | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the trusted network (core members, partners and economies treated as allied), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run

**s1.3** Its distinctive contribution is clean-energy equipment, where it ranks in the top quarter of suppliers although its share of world supply is small (2% in wind turbines, 2% in PV modules and inverters).
